commit 3fc579e24a3a5b161b36dfcca8c0270200a44ed3
Author: Dave <Dave@mail.com>
Date:   Thu Feb 19 05:47:51 2026 +0000

    Dave: Prove ex67_4b_free_abelian_no_torsion, collect bounty 37.
    
    Proves that in a free abelian group with basis, every nonidentity element
    has infinite order (no positive m with a^m = e).
    
    Strategy: Use the direct sum uniqueness property. If a^m = e, then writing
    a as product over basis cyclic subgroups, each component must be e, forcing
    a = e, contradiction.
    
    Key auxiliary lemma proved:
    - group_power_nat_of_power_nat: (a^k)^m = a^(k*m) in any group
    
    Also removes Charlie's expired lock on ex53_2_unique_partition.
    
    Balance: Dave 1597 -> 1634 (bounty 37 collected)
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>
